    def test_production_similarities_reach_the_model_and_unrelated_chunks_do_not():
        """RAG-4: the configured cut keeps every chunk at production similarity.
    
        On production text-embedding-3-large gave the chunks that answer a question
        cosines of 0.425-0.664, unrelated chunks 0.399 at most and off-topic questions
        about 0.15, so the old 0.7 cut let only compression's one survivor through.
        The code default is what production runs: Railway does not set the variable.
        """
        threshold = Settings.model_fields["rag_context_threshold"].default
        query = [1.0, 0.0]
    
        def chunk(chunk_id, cosine):
            return {
                "chunk_id": chunk_id,
                "content": "A" * 400,
                "embedding": [cosine, math.sqrt(1 - cosine**2)],
            }

            [unrelated, *relevant], query_embedding=query, threshold=threshold
        )

	id CHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	from_urn VARCHAR(512) NOT NULL, 
	to_urn VARCHAR(512) NOT NULL, 
	edge_type VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL, 
	direction VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL, 
	weight FLOAT NOT NULL, 
	reason TEXT, 
	via VARCHAR(50), 
	evidence JSON, 
	created_at DATETIME NOT NULL, 
	updated_at DATETIME NOT NULL, 
	PRIMARY KEY (id), 
	CONSTRAINT uq_graph_edges_from_to_type_direction UNIQUE (from_urn, to_urn, edge_type, direction)
)

	id CHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	name VARCHAR NOT NULL, 
	description TEXT, 
	user_id CHAR(36), 
	mission_protocol_id CHAR(36),

	research_type VARCHAR,

CREATE TABLE ledger_sources (
	id CHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	project_id CHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	source_url TEXT NOT NULL, 
	source_url_hash VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL, 
	sighting_count INTEGER DEFAULT 1 NOT NULL, 
	first_seen_at DATETIME DEFAULT (CURRENT_TIMESTAMP) NOT NULL, 
	last_seen_at DATETIME DEFAULT (CURRENT_TIMESTAMP) NOT NULL, 
	PRIMARY KEY (id), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_ledger_sources_nonempty_url CHECK (length(trim(source_url)) > 0), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_ledger_sources_hash_length CHECK (length(source_url_hash) = 64), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_ledger_sources_positive_sightings CHECK (sighting_count > 0), 
	CONSTRAINT uq_ledger_sources_project_url_hash UNIQUE (project_id, source_url_hash), 
	CONSTRAINT uq_ledger_sources_id_project UNIQUE (id, project_id), 
	FOREIGN KEY(project_id) REFERENCES projects (id) ON DELETE CASCADE
)

CREATE TABLE missions (
	id CHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	project_id CHAR(36), 
	mission_id VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL, 
	title VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL, 
	objective TEXT NOT NULL, 
	success_criteria JSON NOT NULL, 
	context JSON, 
	deliverables JSON, 
	research_phases JSON, 
	tags JSON, 
	mission_metadata JSON, 
	background TEXT, 
	focus TEXT, 
	"references" JSON, 
	required_entities JSON, 
	excluded_entities JSON, 
	expected_output_schema JSON, 
	coverage_thresholds JSON, 
	validation_thresholds JSON, 
	deliverable_format TEXT, 
	max_loops INTEGER, 
	min_loops INTEGER, 
	constraints JSON, 
	status VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL, 
	queued_at DATETIME, 
	started_at DATETIME, 
	completed_at DATETIME, 
	deepsearch_job_id VARCHAR(100), 
	deepsearch_lease_owner TEXT, 
	deepsearch_lease_token TEXT, 
	deepsearch_leased_at DATETIME, 
	deepsearch_heartbeat_at DATETIME, 
	deepsearch_lease_expires_at DATETIME, 
	deepsearch_attempt_count INTEGER DEFAULT '0' NOT NULL, 
	deepsearch_result_key TEXT, 
	execution_metadata JSON, 
	result_document_ids JSON, 
	result_report_id CHAR(36), 
	result_markdown TEXT, 
	result_protocol JSON, 
	error_message TEXT, 
	created_at DATETIME NOT NULL, 
	updated_at DATETIME NOT NULL, 
	created_by VARCHAR(100), 
	owner_id CHAR(36), 
	workspace_id CHAR(36), 
	PRIMARY KEY (id), 
	CONSTRAINT success_criteria_not_empty CHECK (jsonb_array_length(success_criteria) > 0), 
	CONSTRAINT title_length CHECK (length(title) >= 3 AND length(title) <= 255), 
	CONSTRAINT valid_mission_status CHECK (status IN ('draft', 'queued', 'in_progress', 'completed', 'blocked', 'cancelled', 'validation_failed')), 
	FOREIGN KEY(project_id) REFERENCES projects (id) ON DELETE CASCADE, 
	UNIQUE (mission_id), 
	FOREIGN KEY(result_report_id) REFERENCES reports (id) ON DELETE SET NULL, 
	FOREIGN KEY(owner_id) REFERENCES users (id) ON DELETE SET NULL, 
	FOREIGN KEY(workspace_id) REFERENCES workspaces (id) ON DELETE SET NULL
)

	mission_id CHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	deepsearch_job_id VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL, 
	session_key VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL, 
	payload_hash VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL, 
	entry_count INTEGER NOT NULL, 
	created_at DATETIME DEFAULT (CURRENT_TIMESTAMP) NOT NULL, 
	updated_at DATETIME DEFAULT (CURRENT_TIMESTAMP) NOT NULL, 
	PRIMARY KEY (id), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_ledger_batches_nonempty_job CHECK (length(trim(deepsearch_job_id)) > 0), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_ledger_batches_nonempty_session CHECK (length(trim(session_key)) > 0), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_ledger_batches_hash_length CHECK (length(payload_hash) = 64), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_ledger_batches_entry_count CHECK (entry_count > 0 AND entry_count <= 1000), 
	CONSTRAINT uq_deepsearch_ledger_batches_mission_job UNIQUE (mission_id, deepsearch_job_id), 
	CONSTRAINT fk_deepsearch_ledger_batches_mission FOREIGN KEY(mission_id) REFERENCES missions (id) ON DELETE CASCADE
)

CREATE TABLE deepsearch_evidence_outbox (
	mission_id CHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	deepsearch_job_id VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL, 
	deepsearch_result_key TEXT NOT NULL, 
	mission_attempt_count INTEGER NOT NULL, 
	terminal_status VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL, 
	schema_version SMALLINT DEFAULT 1 NOT NULL, 
	state VARCHAR(16) DEFAULT 'pending' NOT NULL, 
	delivery_attempt_count INTEGER DEFAULT 0 NOT NULL, 
	next_attempt_at DATETIME DEFAULT (CURRENT_TIMESTAMP) NOT NULL, 
	lease_token CHAR(36), 
	lease_expires_at DATETIME, 
	acked_at DATETIME, 
	last_http_status SMALLINT, 
	last_error_code VARCHAR(100), 
	created_at DATETIME DEFAULT (CURRENT_TIMESTAMP) NOT NULL, 
	updated_at DATETIME DEFAULT (CURRENT_TIMESTAMP) NOT NULL, 
	CONSTRAINT pk_deepsearch_evidence_outbox PRIMARY KEY (mission_id, deepsearch_job_id), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_nonempty_job CHECK (length(trim(deepsearch_job_id)) > 0), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_nonempty_result_key CHECK (length(trim(deepsearch_result_key)) > 0), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_positive_attempt CHECK (mission_attempt_count > 0), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_terminal_status CHECK (terminal_status IN ('completed', 'validation_failed')), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_schema_version CHECK (schema_version = 1), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_state CHECK (state IN ('pending', 'leased', 'acked', 'dead_letter')), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_delivery_attempts CHECK (delivery_attempt_count >= 0), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_http_status CHECK (last_http_status IS NULL OR (last_http_status >= 100 AND last_http_status <= 599)), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_state_coherence CHECK ((state = 'leased' AND lease_token IS NOT NULL AND lease_expires_at IS NOT NULL AND next_attempt_at = lease_expires_at AND acked_at IS NULL) OR (state = 'acked' AND lease_token IS NULL AND lease_expires_at IS NULL AND acked_at IS NOT NULL) OR (state IN ('pending', 'dead_letter') AND lease_token IS NULL AND lease_expires_at IS NULL AND acked_at IS NULL)), 
	CONSTRAINT fk_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_mission FOREIGN KEY(mission_id) REFERENCES missions (id) ON DELETE CASCADE
)

CREATE TABLE ledger_entries (
	id CHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	project_id CHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	mission_id CHAR(36), 
	deepsearch_batch_id CHAR(36), 
	session_key VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL, 
	origin VARCHAR(32) DEFAULT 'mcp-agent' NOT NULL, 
	claim TEXT NOT NULL, 
	summary TEXT, 
	source_url TEXT NOT NULL, 
	source_id CHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	snippet TEXT, 
	"query" TEXT, 
	disposition VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL, 
	tags JSON DEFAULT '[]' NOT NULL, 
	owner_id CHAR(36), 
	workspace_id CHAR(36), 
	created_at DATETIME DEFAULT (CURRENT_TIMESTAMP) NOT NULL, 
	updated_at DATETIME DEFAULT (CURRENT_TIMESTAMP) NOT NULL, 
	PRIMARY KEY (id), 
	CONSTRAINT fk_ledger_entries_source_project FOREIGN KEY(source_id, project_id) REFERENCES ledger_sources (id, project_id) ON DELETE NO ACTION DEFERRABLE INITIALLY DEFERRED, 
	CONSTRAINT ck_ledger_entries_origin CHECK (origin IN ('mcp-agent', 'deepsearch-worker')), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_ledger_entries_disposition CHECK (disposition IN ('supporting', 'contradicting', 'rejected', 'background')), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_ledger_entries_nonempty_session CHECK (length(trim(session_key)) > 0), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_ledger_entries_nonempty_claim CHECK (length(trim(claim)) > 0), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_ledger_entries_nonempty_source_url CHECK (length(trim(source_url)) > 0), 
	FOREIGN KEY(project_id) REFERENCES projects (id) ON DELETE CASCADE,

	id CHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	from_urn VARCHAR(512) NOT NULL, 
	to_urn VARCHAR(512) NOT NULL,

	weight FLOAT NOT NULL, 
	reason TEXT,

	updated_at DATETIME NOT NULL, 
	PRIMARY KEY (id),

)

CREATE TABLE ledger_sources (
	id CHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	project_id CHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	source_url TEXT NOT NULL, 
	source_url_hash VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL, 
	sighting_count INTEGER DEFAULT 1 NOT NULL, 
	first_seen_at DATETIME DEFAULT (CURRENT_TIMESTAMP) NOT NULL, 
	last_seen_at DATETIME DEFAULT (CURRENT_TIMESTAMP) NOT NULL, 
	PRIMARY KEY (id), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_ledger_sources_nonempty_url CHECK (length(trim(source_url)) > 0), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_ledger_sources_hash_length CHECK (length(source_url_hash) = 64), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_ledger_sources_positive_sightings CHECK (sighting_count > 0), 
	CONSTRAINT uq_ledger_sources_project_url_hash UNIQUE (project_id, source_url_hash), 
	CONSTRAINT uq_ledger_sources_id_project UNIQUE (id, project_id), 
	FOREIGN KEY(project_id) REFERENCES projects (id) ON DELETE CASCADE
)

CREATE TABLE missions (
	id CHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	project_id CHAR(36), 
	mission_id VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL, 
	title VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL, 
	objective TEXT NOT NULL, 
	success_criteria JSON NOT NULL, 
	context JSON, 
	deliverables JSON, 
	research_phases JSON, 
	tags JSON, 
	mission_metadata JSON, 
	background TEXT, 
	focus TEXT, 
	"references" JSON, 
	required_entities JSON, 
	excluded_entities JSON, 
	expected_output_schema JSON, 
	coverage_thresholds JSON, 
	validation_thresholds JSON, 
	deliverable_format TEXT, 
	max_loops INTEGER, 
	min_loops INTEGER, 
	constraints JSON, 
	status VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL, 
	queued_at DATETIME, 
	started_at DATETIME, 
	completed_at DATETIME, 
	deepsearch_job_id VARCHAR(100), 
	deepsearch_lease_owner TEXT, 
	deepsearch_lease_token TEXT, 
	deepsearch_leased_at DATETIME, 
	deepsearch_heartbeat_at DATETIME, 
	deepsearch_lease_expires_at DATETIME, 
	deepsearch_attempt_count INTEGER DEFAULT '0' NOT NULL, 
	deepsearch_result_key TEXT, 
	execution_metadata JSON, 
	result_document_ids JSON, 
	result_report_id CHAR(36), 
	result_markdown TEXT, 
	result_protocol JSON, 
	error_message TEXT, 
	created_at DATETIME NOT NULL, 
	updated_at DATETIME NOT NULL, 
	created_by VARCHAR(100), 
	owner_id CHAR(36), 
	workspace_id CHAR(36), 
	PRIMARY KEY (id), 
	CONSTRAINT success_criteria_not_empty CHECK (jsonb_array_length(success_criteria) > 0), 
	CONSTRAINT title_length CHECK (length(title) >= 3 AND length(title) <= 255), 
	CONSTRAINT valid_mission_status CHECK (status IN ('draft', 'queued', 'in_progress', 'completed', 'blocked', 'cancelled', 'validation_failed')), 
	FOREIGN KEY(project_id) REFERENCES projects (id) ON DELETE CASCADE, 
	UNIQUE (mission_id), 
	FOREIGN KEY(result_report_id) REFERENCES reports (id) ON DELETE SET NULL, 
	FOREIGN KEY(owner_id) REFERENCES users (id) ON DELETE SET NULL, 
	FOREIGN KEY(workspace_id) REFERENCES workspaces (id) ON DELETE SET NULL
)

CREATE TABLE deepsearch_evidence_outbox (
	mission_id CHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	deepsearch_job_id VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL, 
	deepsearch_result_key TEXT NOT NULL, 
	mission_attempt_count INTEGER NOT NULL, 
	terminal_status VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL, 
	schema_version SMALLINT DEFAULT 1 NOT NULL, 
	state VARCHAR(16) DEFAULT 'pending' NOT NULL, 
	delivery_attempt_count INTEGER DEFAULT 0 NOT NULL, 
	next_attempt_at DATETIME DEFAULT (CURRENT_TIMESTAMP) NOT NULL, 
	lease_token CHAR(36), 
	lease_expires_at DATETIME, 
	acked_at DATETIME, 
	last_http_status SMALLINT, 
	last_error_code VARCHAR(100), 
	created_at DATETIME DEFAULT (CURRENT_TIMESTAMP) NOT NULL, 
	updated_at DATETIME DEFAULT (CURRENT_TIMESTAMP) NOT NULL, 
	CONSTRAINT pk_deepsearch_evidence_outbox PRIMARY KEY (mission_id, deepsearch_job_id), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_nonempty_job CHECK (length(trim(deepsearch_job_id)) > 0), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_nonempty_result_key CHECK (length(trim(deepsearch_result_key)) > 0), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_positive_attempt CHECK (mission_attempt_count > 0), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_terminal_status CHECK (terminal_status IN ('completed', 'validation_failed')), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_schema_version CHECK (schema_version = 1), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_state CHECK (state IN ('pending', 'leased', 'acked', 'dead_letter')), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_delivery_attempts CHECK (delivery_attempt_count >= 0), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_http_status CHECK (last_http_status IS NULL OR (last_http_status >= 100 AND last_http_status <= 599)), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_state_coherence CHECK ((state = 'leased' AND lease_token IS NOT NULL AND lease_expires_at IS NOT NULL AND next_attempt_at = lease_expires_at AND acked_at IS NULL) OR (state = 'acked' AND lease_token IS NULL AND lease_expires_at IS NULL AND acked_at IS NOT NULL) OR (state IN ('pending', 'dead_letter') AND lease_token IS NULL AND lease_expires_at IS NULL AND acked_at IS NULL)), 
	CONSTRAINT fk_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_mission FOREIGN KEY(mission_id) REFERENCES missions (id) ON DELETE CASCADE
)

CREATE TABLE ledger_entries (
	id CHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	project_id CHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	mission_id CHAR(36), 
	deepsearch_batch_id CHAR(36), 
	session_key VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL, 
	origin VARCHAR(32) DEFAULT 'mcp-agent' NOT NULL, 
	claim TEXT NOT NULL, 
	summary TEXT, 
	source_url TEXT NOT NULL, 
	source_id CHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	snippet TEXT, 
	"query" TEXT, 
	disposition VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL, 
	tags JSON DEFAULT '[]' NOT NULL, 
	owner_id CHAR(36), 
	workspace_id CHAR(36), 
	created_at DATETIME DEFAULT (CURRENT_TIMESTAMP) NOT NULL, 
	updated_at DATETIME DEFAULT (CURRENT_TIMESTAMP) NOT NULL, 
	PRIMARY KEY (id), 
	CONSTRAINT fk_ledger_entries_source_project FOREIGN KEY(source_id, project_id) REFERENCES ledger_sources (id, project_id) ON DELETE NO ACTION DEFERRABLE INITIALLY DEFERRED, 
	CONSTRAINT ck_ledger_entries_origin CHECK (origin IN ('mcp-agent', 'deepsearch-worker')), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_ledger_entries_disposition CHECK (disposition IN ('supporting', 'contradicting', 'rejected', 'background')), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_ledger_entries_nonempty_session CHECK (length(trim(session_key)) > 0), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_ledger_entries_nonempty_claim CHECK (length(trim(claim)) > 0), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_ledger_entries_nonempty_source_url CHECK (length(trim(source_url)) > 0), 
	FOREIGN KEY(project_id) REFERENCES projects (id) ON DELETE CASCADE, 
	FOREIGN KEY(mission_id) REFERENCES missions (id) ON DELETE SET NULL, 
	CONSTRAINT fk_ledger_entries_deepsearch_batch FOREIGN KEY(deepsearch_batch_id) REFERENCES deepsearch_ledger_batches (id) ON DELETE SET NULL, 
	FOREIGN KEY(owner_id) REFERENCES users (id) ON DELETE SET NULL, 
	FOREIGN KEY(workspace_id) REFERENCES workspaces (id) ON DELETE SET NULL
)

CREATE TABLE graph_edges (
	id CHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	from_urn VARCHAR(512) NOT NULL, 
	to_urn VARCHAR(512) NOT NULL, 
	edge_type VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,

	reason TEXT,

	evidence JSON,

	updated_at DATETIME NOT NULL,

)

CREATE TABLE missions (
	id CHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	project_id CHAR(36), 
	mission_id VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL, 
	title VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL, 
	objective TEXT NOT NULL, 
	success_criteria JSON NOT NULL, 
	context JSON, 
	deliverables JSON, 
	research_phases JSON, 
	tags JSON, 
	mission_metadata JSON, 
	background TEXT, 
	focus TEXT, 
	"references" JSON, 
	required_entities JSON, 
	excluded_entities JSON, 
	expected_output_schema JSON, 
	coverage_thresholds JSON, 
	validation_thresholds JSON, 
	deliverable_format TEXT, 
	max_loops INTEGER, 
	min_loops INTEGER, 
	constraints JSON, 
	status VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL, 
	queued_at DATETIME, 
	started_at DATETIME, 
	completed_at DATETIME, 
	deepsearch_job_id VARCHAR(100), 
	deepsearch_lease_owner TEXT, 
	deepsearch_lease_token TEXT, 
	deepsearch_leased_at DATETIME, 
	deepsearch_heartbeat_at DATETIME, 
	deepsearch_lease_expires_at DATETIME, 
	deepsearch_attempt_count INTEGER DEFAULT '0' NOT NULL, 
	deepsearch_result_key TEXT, 
	execution_metadata JSON, 
	result_document_ids JSON, 
	result_report_id CHAR(36), 
	result_markdown TEXT, 
	result_protocol JSON, 
	error_message TEXT, 
	created_at DATETIME NOT NULL, 
	updated_at DATETIME NOT NULL, 
	created_by VARCHAR(100), 
	owner_id CHAR(36), 
	workspace_id CHAR(36), 
	PRIMARY KEY (id), 
	CONSTRAINT success_criteria_not_empty CHECK (jsonb_array_length(success_criteria) > 0), 
	CONSTRAINT title_length CHECK (length(title) >= 3 AND length(title) <= 255), 
	CONSTRAINT valid_mission_status CHECK (status IN ('draft', 'queued', 'in_progress', 'completed', 'blocked', 'cancelled', 'validation_failed')), 
	FOREIGN KEY(project_id) REFERENCES projects (id) ON DELETE CASCADE,

	FOREIGN KEY(owner_id) REFERENCES users (id) ON DELETE SET NULL, 
	FOREIGN KEY(workspace_id) REFERENCES workspaces (id) ON DELETE SET NULL

CREATE TABLE deepsearch_evidence_outbox (
	mission_id CHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	deepsearch_job_id VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL, 
	deepsearch_result_key TEXT NOT NULL, 
	mission_attempt_count INTEGER NOT NULL, 
	terminal_status VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL, 
	schema_version SMALLINT DEFAULT 1 NOT NULL, 
	state VARCHAR(16) DEFAULT 'pending' NOT NULL, 
	delivery_attempt_count INTEGER DEFAULT 0 NOT NULL, 
	next_attempt_at DATETIME DEFAULT (CURRENT_TIMESTAMP) NOT NULL, 
	lease_token CHAR(36), 
	lease_expires_at DATETIME, 
	acked_at DATETIME, 
	last_http_status SMALLINT, 
	last_error_code VARCHAR(100), 
	created_at DATETIME DEFAULT (CURRENT_TIMESTAMP) NOT NULL, 
	updated_at DATETIME DEFAULT (CURRENT_TIMESTAMP) NOT NULL, 
	CONSTRAINT pk_deepsearch_evidence_outbox PRIMARY KEY (mission_id, deepsearch_job_id), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_nonempty_job CHECK (length(trim(deepsearch_job_id)) > 0), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_nonempty_result_key CHECK (length(trim(deepsearch_result_key)) > 0), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_positive_attempt CHECK (mission_attempt_count > 0), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_terminal_status CHECK (terminal_status IN ('completed', 'validation_failed')), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_schema_version CHECK (schema_version = 1), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_state CHECK (state IN ('pending', 'leased', 'acked', 'dead_letter')), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_delivery_attempts CHECK (delivery_attempt_count >= 0), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_http_status CHECK (last_http_status IS NULL OR (last_http_status >= 100 AND last_http_status <= 599)),

	CONSTRAINT fk_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_mission FOREIGN KEY(mission_id) REFERENCES missions (id) ON DELETE CASCADE
)

CREATE TABLE ledger_entries (
	id CHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	project_id CHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	mission_id CHAR(36), 
	deepsearch_batch_id CHAR(36), 
	session_key VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL, 
	origin VARCHAR(32) DEFAULT 'mcp-agent' NOT NULL, 
	claim TEXT NOT NULL, 
	summary TEXT, 
	source_url TEXT NOT NULL, 
	source_id CHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	snippet TEXT, 
	"query" TEXT, 
	disposition VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL, 
	tags JSON DEFAULT '[]' NOT NULL, 
	owner_id CHAR(36), 
	workspace_id CHAR(36), 
	created_at DATETIME DEFAULT (CURRENT_TIMESTAMP) NOT NULL, 
	updated_at DATETIME DEFAULT (CURRENT_TIMESTAMP) NOT NULL, 
	PRIMARY KEY (id), 
	CONSTRAINT fk_ledger_entries_source_project FOREIGN KEY(source_id, project_id) REFERENCES ledger_sources (id, project_id) ON DELETE NO ACTION DEFERRABLE INITIALLY DEFERRED, 
	CONSTRAINT ck_ledger_entries_origin CHECK (origin IN ('mcp-agent', 'deepsearch-worker')), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_ledger_entries_disposition CHECK (disposition IN ('supporting', 'contradicting', 'rejected', 'background')), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_ledger_entries_nonempty_session CHECK (length(trim(session_key)) > 0), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_ledger_entries_nonempty_claim CHECK (length(trim(claim)) > 0), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_ledger_entries_nonempty_source_url CHECK (length(trim(source_url)) > 0), 
	FOREIGN KEY(project_id) REFERENCES projects (id) ON DELETE CASCADE, 
	FOREIGN KEY(mission_id) REFERENCES missions (id) ON DELETE SET NULL, 
	CONSTRAINT fk_ledger_entries_deepsearch_batch FOREIGN KEY(deepsearch_batch_id) REFERENCES deepsearch_ledger_batches (id) ON DELETE SET NULL, 
	FOREIGN KEY(owner_id) REFERENCES users (id) ON DELETE SET NULL, 
	FOREIGN KEY(workspace_id) REFERENCES workspaces (id) ON DELETE SET NULL
)

	id CHAR(36) NOT NULL,

	to_urn VARCHAR(512) NOT NULL,

)

	id CHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	project_id CHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	source_url TEXT NOT NULL, 
	source_url_hash VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL, 
	sighting_count INTEGER DEFAULT 1 NOT NULL,

	last_seen_at DATETIME DEFAULT (CURRENT_TIMESTAMP) NOT NULL,

	id CHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	project_id CHAR(36), 
	title VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL, 
	report_type VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL, 
	prompt TEXT, 
	content TEXT NOT NULL, 
	content_hash VARCHAR(64), 
	version INTEGER NOT NULL, 
	parent_id CHAR(36), 
	status VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL, 
	tokens_used INTEGER NOT NULL, 
	chunk_count INTEGER NOT NULL, 
	created_at DATETIME NOT NULL, 
	updated_at DATETIME NOT NULL, 
	created_by VARCHAR(100), 
	owner_id CHAR(36), 
	workspace_id CHAR(36), 
	PRIMARY KEY (id), 
	FOREIGN KEY(project_id) REFERENCES projects (id) ON DELETE SET NULL, 
	FOREIGN KEY(parent_id) REFERENCES reports (id) ON DELETE SET NULL, 
	FOREIGN KEY(owner_id) REFERENCES users (id) ON DELETE SET NULL, 
	FOREIGN KEY(workspace_id) REFERENCES workspaces (id) ON DELETE SET NULL
)

CREATE TABLE missions (
	id CHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	project_id CHAR(36), 
	mission_id VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL, 
	title VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL, 
	objective TEXT NOT NULL, 
	success_criteria JSON NOT NULL, 
	context JSON, 
	deliverables JSON, 
	research_phases JSON, 
	tags JSON, 
	mission_metadata JSON, 
	background TEXT, 
	focus TEXT, 
	"references" JSON, 
	required_entities JSON, 
	excluded_entities JSON, 
	expected_output_schema JSON, 
	coverage_thresholds JSON, 
	validation_thresholds JSON, 
	deliverable_format TEXT, 
	max_loops INTEGER, 
	min_loops INTEGER, 
	constraints JSON, 
	status VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL, 
	queued_at DATETIME, 
	started_at DATETIME, 
	completed_at DATETIME, 
	deepsearch_job_id VARCHAR(100), 
	deepsearch_lease_owner TEXT, 
	deepsearch_lease_token TEXT, 
	deepsearch_leased_at DATETIME, 
	deepsearch_heartbeat_at DATETIME, 
	deepsearch_lease_expires_at DATETIME, 
	deepsearch_attempt_count INTEGER DEFAULT '0' NOT NULL, 
	deepsearch_result_key TEXT, 
	execution_metadata JSON, 
	result_document_ids JSON, 
	result_report_id CHAR(36), 
	result_markdown TEXT, 
	result_protocol JSON, 
	error_message TEXT, 
	created_at DATETIME NOT NULL, 
	updated_at DATETIME NOT NULL, 
	created_by VARCHAR(100), 
	owner_id CHAR(36), 
	workspace_id CHAR(36), 
	PRIMARY KEY (id), 
	CONSTRAINT success_criteria_not_empty CHECK (jsonb_array_length(success_criteria) > 0), 
	CONSTRAINT title_length CHECK (length(title) >= 3 AND length(title) <= 255), 
	CONSTRAINT valid_mission_status CHECK (status IN ('draft', 'queued', 'in_progress', 'completed', 'blocked', 'cancelled', 'validation_failed')), 
	FOREIGN KEY(project_id) REFERENCES projects (id) ON DELETE CASCADE, 
	UNIQUE (mission_id), 
	FOREIGN KEY(result_report_id) REFERENCES reports (id) ON DELETE SET NULL, 
	FOREIGN KEY(owner_id) REFERENCES users (id) ON DELETE SET NULL, 
	FOREIGN KEY(workspace_id) REFERENCES workspaces (id) ON DELETE SET NULL
)

	id CHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	mission_id CHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	deepsearch_job_id VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL, 
	session_key VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL, 
	payload_hash VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL, 
	entry_count INTEGER NOT NULL, 
	created_at DATETIME DEFAULT (CURRENT_TIMESTAMP) NOT NULL, 
	updated_at DATETIME DEFAULT (CURRENT_TIMESTAMP) NOT NULL, 
	PRIMARY KEY (id), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_ledger_batches_nonempty_job CHECK (length(trim(deepsearch_job_id)) > 0), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_ledger_batches_nonempty_session CHECK (length(trim(session_key)) > 0), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_ledger_batches_hash_length CHECK (length(payload_hash) = 64), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_ledger_batches_entry_count CHECK (entry_count > 0 AND entry_count <= 1000), 
	CONSTRAINT uq_deepsearch_ledger_batches_mission_job UNIQUE (mission_id, deepsearch_job_id), 
	CONSTRAINT fk_deepsearch_ledger_batches_mission FOREIGN KEY(mission_id) REFERENCES missions (id) ON DELETE CASCADE
)

CREATE TABLE deepsearch_evidence_outbox (
	mission_id CHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	deepsearch_job_id VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL, 
	deepsearch_result_key TEXT NOT NULL, 
	mission_attempt_count INTEGER NOT NULL, 
	terminal_status VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL, 
	schema_version SMALLINT DEFAULT 1 NOT NULL, 
	state VARCHAR(16) DEFAULT 'pending' NOT NULL, 
	delivery_attempt_count INTEGER DEFAULT 0 NOT NULL, 
	next_attempt_at DATETIME DEFAULT (CURRENT_TIMESTAMP) NOT NULL, 
	lease_token CHAR(36), 
	lease_expires_at DATETIME, 
	acked_at DATETIME, 
	last_http_status SMALLINT, 
	last_error_code VARCHAR(100), 
	created_at DATETIME DEFAULT (CURRENT_TIMESTAMP) NOT NULL, 
	updated_at DATETIME DEFAULT (CURRENT_TIMESTAMP) NOT NULL, 
	CONSTRAINT pk_deepsearch_evidence_outbox PRIMARY KEY (mission_id, deepsearch_job_id), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_nonempty_job CHECK (length(trim(deepsearch_job_id)) > 0), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_nonempty_result_key CHECK (length(trim(deepsearch_result_key)) > 0), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_positive_attempt CHECK (mission_attempt_count > 0), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_terminal_status CHECK (terminal_status IN ('completed', 'validation_failed')), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_schema_version CHECK (schema_version = 1), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_state CHECK (state IN ('pending', 'leased', 'acked', 'dead_letter')), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_delivery_attempts CHECK (delivery_attempt_count >= 0), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_http_status CHECK (last_http_status IS NULL OR (last_http_status >= 100 AND last_http_status <= 599)), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_state_coherence CHECK ((state = 'leased' AND lease_token IS NOT NULL AND lease_expires_at IS NOT NULL AND next_attempt_at = lease_expires_at AND acked_at IS NULL) OR (state = 'acked' AND lease_token IS NULL AND lease_expires_at IS NULL AND acked_at IS NOT NULL) OR (state IN ('pending', 'dead_letter') AND lease_token IS NULL AND lease_expires_at IS NULL AND acked_at IS NULL)), 
	CONSTRAINT fk_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_mission FOREIGN KEY(mission_id) REFERENCES missions (id) ON DELETE CASCADE
)

CREATE TABLE ledger_entries (
	id CHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	project_id CHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	mission_id CHAR(36), 
	deepsearch_batch_id CHAR(36), 
	session_key VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL, 
	origin VARCHAR(32) DEFAULT 'mcp-agent' NOT NULL, 
	claim TEXT NOT NULL, 
	summary TEXT, 
	source_url TEXT NOT NULL, 
	source_id CHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	snippet TEXT, 
	"query" TEXT, 
	disposition VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL, 
	tags JSON DEFAULT '[]' NOT NULL, 
	owner_id CHAR(36), 
	workspace_id CHAR(36), 
	created_at DATETIME DEFAULT (CURRENT_TIMESTAMP) NOT NULL, 
	updated_at DATETIME DEFAULT (CURRENT_TIMESTAMP) NOT NULL, 
	PRIMARY KEY (id), 
	CONSTRAINT fk_ledger_entries_source_project FOREIGN KEY(source_id, project_id) REFERENCES ledger_sources (id, project_id) ON DELETE NO ACTION DEFERRABLE INITIALLY DEFERRED, 
	CONSTRAINT ck_ledger_entries_origin CHECK (origin IN ('mcp-agent', 'deepsearch-worker')), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_ledger_entries_disposition CHECK (disposition IN ('supporting', 'contradicting', 'rejected', 'background')), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_ledger_entries_nonempty_session CHECK (length(trim(session_key)) > 0), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_ledger_entries_nonempty_claim CHECK (length(trim(claim)) > 0), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_ledger_entries_nonempty_source_url CHECK (length(trim(source_url)) > 0), 
	FOREIGN KEY(project_id) REFERENCES projects (id) ON DELETE CASCADE, 
	FOREIGN KEY(mission_id) REFERENCES missions (id) ON DELETE SET NULL, 
	CONSTRAINT fk_ledger_entries_deepsearch_batch FOREIGN KEY(deepsearch_batch_id) REFERENCES deepsearch_ledger_batches (id) ON DELETE SET NULL, 
	FOREIGN KEY(owner_id) REFERENCES users (id) ON DELETE SET NULL, 
	FOREIGN KEY(workspace_id) REFERENCES workspaces (id) ON DELETE SET NULL
)

CREATE TABLE graph_edges (
	id CHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	from_urn VARCHAR(512) NOT NULL, 
	to_urn VARCHAR(512) NOT NULL, 
	edge_type VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL, 
	direction VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL, 
	weight FLOAT NOT NULL, 
	reason TEXT, 
	via VARCHAR(50), 
	evidence JSON, 
	created_at DATETIME NOT NULL, 
	updated_at DATETIME NOT NULL, 
	PRIMARY KEY (id), 
	CONSTRAINT uq_graph_edges_from_to_type_direction UNIQUE (from_urn, to_urn, edge_type, direction)
)

CREATE TABLE projects (
	id CHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	name VARCHAR NOT NULL, 
	description TEXT, 
	user_id CHAR(36), 
	mission_protocol_id CHAR(36), 
	owner_id CHAR(36), 
	workspace_id CHAR(36), 
	research_type VARCHAR, 
	methodology VARCHAR, 
	status VARCHAR, 
	quality_score INTEGER, 
	last_quality_check DATETIME, 
	created_at DATETIME, 
	updated_at DATETIME, 
	deleted_at DATETIME, 
	deleted_by VARCHAR(100),

CREATE TABLE missions (
	id CHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	project_id CHAR(36), 
	mission_id VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL, 
	title VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL, 
	objective TEXT NOT NULL, 
	success_criteria JSON NOT NULL, 
	context JSON, 
	deliverables JSON, 
	research_phases JSON, 
	tags JSON, 
	mission_metadata JSON, 
	background TEXT, 
	focus TEXT, 
	"references" JSON, 
	required_entities JSON, 
	excluded_entities JSON, 
	expected_output_schema JSON, 
	coverage_thresholds JSON, 
	validation_thresholds JSON, 
	deliverable_format TEXT, 
	max_loops INTEGER, 
	min_loops INTEGER, 
	constraints JSON, 
	status VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL, 
	queued_at DATETIME, 
	started_at DATETIME, 
	completed_at DATETIME, 
	deepsearch_job_id VARCHAR(100), 
	deepsearch_lease_owner TEXT, 
	deepsearch_lease_token TEXT, 
	deepsearch_leased_at DATETIME, 
	deepsearch_heartbeat_at DATETIME, 
	deepsearch_lease_expires_at DATETIME, 
	deepsearch_attempt_count INTEGER DEFAULT '0' NOT NULL, 
	deepsearch_result_key TEXT, 
	execution_metadata JSON, 
	result_document_ids JSON, 
	result_report_id CHAR(36), 
	result_markdown TEXT, 
	result_protocol JSON, 
	error_message TEXT, 
	created_at DATETIME NOT NULL, 
	updated_at DATETIME NOT NULL, 
	created_by VARCHAR(100), 
	owner_id CHAR(36), 
	workspace_id CHAR(36), 
	PRIMARY KEY (id), 
	CONSTRAINT success_criteria_not_empty CHECK (jsonb_array_length(success_criteria) > 0), 
	CONSTRAINT title_length CHECK (length(title) >= 3 AND length(title) <= 255), 
	CONSTRAINT valid_mission_status CHECK (status IN ('draft', 'queued', 'in_progress', 'completed', 'blocked', 'cancelled', 'validation_failed')),

CREATE TABLE deepsearch_evidence_outbox (
	mission_id CHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	deepsearch_job_id VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL, 
	deepsearch_result_key TEXT NOT NULL, 
	mission_attempt_count INTEGER NOT NULL, 
	terminal_status VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL, 
	schema_version SMALLINT DEFAULT 1 NOT NULL, 
	state VARCHAR(16) DEFAULT 'pending' NOT NULL, 
	delivery_attempt_count INTEGER DEFAULT 0 NOT NULL, 
	next_attempt_at DATETIME DEFAULT (CURRENT_TIMESTAMP) NOT NULL, 
	lease_token CHAR(36), 
	lease_expires_at DATETIME, 
	acked_at DATETIME, 
	last_http_status SMALLINT, 
	last_error_code VARCHAR(100), 
	created_at DATETIME DEFAULT (CURRENT_TIMESTAMP) NOT NULL, 
	updated_at DATETIME DEFAULT (CURRENT_TIMESTAMP) NOT NULL, 
	CONSTRAINT pk_deepsearch_evidence_outbox PRIMARY KEY (mission_id, deepsearch_job_id), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_nonempty_job CHECK (length(trim(deepsearch_job_id)) > 0), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_nonempty_result_key CHECK (length(trim(deepsearch_result_key)) > 0), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_positive_attempt CHECK (mission_attempt_count > 0), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_terminal_status CHECK (terminal_status IN ('completed', 'validation_failed')), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_schema_version CHECK (schema_version = 1), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_state CHECK (state IN ('pending', 'leased', 'acked', 'dead_letter')), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_delivery_attempts CHECK (delivery_attempt_count >= 0), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_http_status CHECK (last_http_status IS NULL OR (last_http_status >= 100 AND last_http_status <= 599)), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_state_coherence CHECK ((state = 'leased' AND lease_token IS NOT NULL AND lease_expires_at IS NOT NULL AND next_attempt_at = lease_expires_at AND acked_at IS NULL) OR (state = 'acked' AND lease_token IS NULL AND lease_expires_at IS NULL AND acked_at IS NOT NULL) OR (state IN ('pending', 'dead_letter') AND lease_token IS NULL AND lease_expires_at IS NULL AND acked_at IS NULL)), 
	CONSTRAINT fk_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_mission FOREIGN KEY(mission_id) REFERENCES missions (id) ON DELETE CASCADE
)

	created_at DATETIME, 
	PRIMARY KEY (id), 
	CONSTRAINT uq_document_chunks_document_index UNIQUE (document_id, chunk_index), 
	FOREIGN KEY(document_id) REFERENCES documents (id) ON DELETE CASCADE, 
	FOREIGN KEY(prev_chunk_id) REFERENCES document_chunks (id), 
	FOREIGN KEY(next_chunk_id) REFERENCES document_chunks (id)
)

CREATE TABLE ledger_entries (
	id CHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	project_id CHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	mission_id CHAR(36), 
	deepsearch_batch_id CHAR(36), 
	session_key VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL, 
	origin VARCHAR(32) DEFAULT 'mcp-agent' NOT NULL, 
	claim TEXT NOT NULL, 
	summary TEXT, 
	source_url TEXT NOT NULL, 
	source_id CHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	snippet TEXT, 
	"query" TEXT, 
	disposition VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL, 
	tags JSON DEFAULT '[]' NOT NULL, 
	owner_id CHAR(36), 
	workspace_id CHAR(36), 
	created_at DATETIME DEFAULT (CURRENT_TIMESTAMP) NOT NULL, 
	updated_at DATETIME DEFAULT (CURRENT_TIMESTAMP) NOT NULL, 
	PRIMARY KEY (id), 
	CONSTRAINT fk_ledger_entries_source_project FOREIGN KEY(source_id, project_id) REFERENCES ledger_sources (id, project_id) ON DELETE NO ACTION DEFERRABLE INITIALLY DEFERRED, 
	CONSTRAINT ck_ledger_entries_origin CHECK (origin IN ('mcp-agent', 'deepsearch-worker')), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_ledger_entries_disposition CHECK (disposition IN ('supporting', 'contradicting', 'rejected', 'background')), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_ledger_entries_nonempty_session CHECK (length(trim(session_key)) > 0), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_ledger_entries_nonempty_claim CHECK (length(trim(claim)) > 0), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_ledger_entries_nonempty_source_url CHECK (length(trim(source_url)) > 0), 
	FOREIGN KEY(project_id) REFERENCES projects (id) ON DELETE CASCADE, 
	FOREIGN KEY(mission_id) REFERENCES missions (id) ON DELETE SET NULL, 
	CONSTRAINT fk_ledger_entries_deepsearch_batch FOREIGN KEY(deepsearch_batch_id) REFERENCES deepsearch_ledger_batches (id) ON DELETE SET NULL, 
	FOREIGN KEY(owner_id) REFERENCES users (id) ON DELETE SET NULL, 
	FOREIGN KEY(workspace_id) REFERENCES workspaces (id) ON DELETE SET NULL
)

	project_id CHAR(36) NOT NULL,

	last_seen_at DATETIME DEFAULT (CURRENT_TIMESTAMP) NOT NULL,

	CONSTRAINT ck_ledger_sources_positive_sightings CHECK (sighting_count > 0),

CREATE TABLE missions (
	id CHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	project_id CHAR(36), 
	mission_id VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL, 
	title VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL, 
	objective TEXT NOT NULL, 
	success_criteria JSON NOT NULL, 
	context JSON, 
	deliverables JSON, 
	research_phases JSON, 
	tags JSON, 
	mission_metadata JSON, 
	background TEXT, 
	focus TEXT, 
	"references" JSON, 
	required_entities JSON, 
	excluded_entities JSON, 
	expected_output_schema JSON, 
	coverage_thresholds JSON, 
	validation_thresholds JSON, 
	deliverable_format TEXT, 
	max_loops INTEGER, 
	min_loops INTEGER, 
	constraints JSON, 
	status VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL, 
	queued_at DATETIME, 
	started_at DATETIME, 
	completed_at DATETIME, 
	deepsearch_job_id VARCHAR(100), 
	deepsearch_lease_owner TEXT, 
	deepsearch_lease_token TEXT, 
	deepsearch_leased_at DATETIME, 
	deepsearch_heartbeat_at DATETIME, 
	deepsearch_lease_expires_at DATETIME, 
	deepsearch_attempt_count INTEGER DEFAULT '0' NOT NULL, 
	deepsearch_result_key TEXT, 
	execution_metadata JSON, 
	result_document_ids JSON, 
	result_report_id CHAR(36), 
	result_markdown TEXT, 
	result_protocol JSON, 
	error_message TEXT, 
	created_at DATETIME NOT NULL, 
	updated_at DATETIME NOT NULL, 
	created_by VARCHAR(100), 
	owner_id CHAR(36), 
	workspace_id CHAR(36), 
	PRIMARY KEY (id), 
	CONSTRAINT success_criteria_not_empty CHECK (jsonb_array_length(success_criteria) > 0), 
	CONSTRAINT title_length CHECK (length(title) >= 3 AND length(title) <= 255), 
	CONSTRAINT valid_mission_status CHECK (status IN ('draft', 'queued', 'in_progress', 'completed', 'blocked', 'cancelled', 'validation_failed')), 
	FOREIGN KEY(project_id) REFERENCES projects (id) ON DELETE CASCADE, 
	UNIQUE (mission_id), 
	FOREIGN KEY(result_report_id) REFERENCES reports (id) ON DELETE SET NULL, 
	FOREIGN KEY(owner_id) REFERENCES users (id) ON DELETE SET NULL, 
	FOREIGN KEY(workspace_id) REFERENCES workspaces (id) ON DELETE SET NULL
)

	updated_at DATETIME DEFAULT (CURRENT_TIMESTAMP) NOT NULL, 
	PRIMARY KEY (id),

	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_ledger_batches_nonempty_session CHECK (length(trim(session_key)) > 0), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_ledger_batches_hash_length CHECK (length(payload_hash) = 64), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_ledger_batches_entry_count CHECK (entry_count > 0 AND entry_count <= 1000), 
	CONSTRAINT uq_deepsearch_ledger_batches_mission_job UNIQUE (mission_id, deepsearch_job_id),

CREATE TABLE deepsearch_evidence_outbox (
	mission_id CHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	deepsearch_job_id VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL, 
	deepsearch_result_key TEXT NOT NULL, 
	mission_attempt_count INTEGER NOT NULL, 
	terminal_status VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL, 
	schema_version SMALLINT DEFAULT 1 NOT NULL, 
	state VARCHAR(16) DEFAULT 'pending' NOT NULL, 
	delivery_attempt_count INTEGER DEFAULT 0 NOT NULL, 
	next_attempt_at DATETIME DEFAULT (CURRENT_TIMESTAMP) NOT NULL, 
	lease_token CHAR(36), 
	lease_expires_at DATETIME, 
	acked_at DATETIME, 
	last_http_status SMALLINT, 
	last_error_code VARCHAR(100), 
	created_at DATETIME DEFAULT (CURRENT_TIMESTAMP) NOT NULL, 
	updated_at DATETIME DEFAULT (CURRENT_TIMESTAMP) NOT NULL, 
	CONSTRAINT pk_deepsearch_evidence_outbox PRIMARY KEY (mission_id, deepsearch_job_id), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_nonempty_job CHECK (length(trim(deepsearch_job_id)) > 0), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_nonempty_result_key CHECK (length(trim(deepsearch_result_key)) > 0), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_positive_attempt CHECK (mission_attempt_count > 0), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_terminal_status CHECK (terminal_status IN ('completed', 'validation_failed')), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_schema_version CHECK (schema_version = 1), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_state CHECK (state IN ('pending', 'leased', 'acked', 'dead_letter')), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_delivery_attempts CHECK (delivery_attempt_count >= 0), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_http_status CHECK (last_http_status IS NULL OR (last_http_status >= 100 AND last_http_status <= 599)), 
	CONSTRAINT ck_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_state_coherence CHECK ((state = 'leased' AND lease_token IS NOT NULL AND lease_expires_at IS NOT NULL AND next_attempt_at = lease_expires_at AND acked_at IS NULL) OR (state = 'acked' AND lease_token IS NULL AND lease_expires_at IS NULL AND acked_at IS NOT NULL) OR (state IN ('pending', 'dead_letter') AND lease_token IS NULL AND lease_expires_at IS NULL AND acked_at IS NULL)), 
	CONSTRAINT fk_deepsearch_evidence_outbox_mission FOREIGN KEY(mission_id) REFERENCES missions (id) ON DELETE CASCADE
)

	id CHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	project_id CHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	mission_id CHAR(36), 
	deepsearch_batch_id CHAR(36), 
	session_key VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL, 
	origin VARCHAR(32) DEFAULT 'mcp-agent' NOT NULL, 
	claim TEXT NOT NULL, 
	summary TEXT, 
	source_url TEXT NOT NULL, 
	source_id CHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	snippet TEXT, 
	"query" TEXT, 
	disposition VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL, 
	tags JSON DEFAULT '[]' NOT NULL, 
	owner_id CHAR(36), 
	workspace_id CHAR(36), 
	created_at DATETIME DEFAULT (CURRENT_TIMESTAMP) NOT NULL, 
	updated_at DATETIME DEFAULT (CURRENT_TIMESTAMP) NOT NULL, 
	PRIMARY KEY (id), 
	CONSTRAINT fk_ledger_entries_source_project FOREIGN KEY(source_id, project_id) REFERENCES ledger_sources (id, project_id) ON DELETE NO ACTION DEFERRABLE INITIALLY DEFERRED, 
	CONSTRAINT ck_ledger_entries_origin CHECK (origin IN ('mcp-agent', 'deepsearch-worker')),

	CONSTRAINT ck_ledger_entries_nonempty_claim CHECK (length(trim(claim)) > 0),

	FOREIGN KEY(project_id) REFERENCES projects (id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
